


===========================
== M A C M - 1 7 1 8 - K ==
===========================




 INITIALIZED FOR:

   DRUM

 WE REMIND YOU OF THE FOLLOWING MACM COMMANDS:

       )REDEF   => REDEFINE DISC TYPE
       )HENT    => GET SINTRAN FROM SAVE-AREA
       22!      => START SINTRAN
       10,0$    => LOAD SINTRAN FROM DISKETTE

 YOU MAY NOW TYPE ANY MACM COMMAND.
 ==================================


)REDEF


PLEASE DEFINE THE DISC TYPE (MSTYP) !

MSTYP  SINTRAN DEVICE NAME

 0   DRUM
 2   DISC-10MB
 3   DISC-66MB AND DISC-33MB
 4   DISC-75MB AND DISC-38MB
 5   DISC-288MB AND DISC-3-75MB
 6   DISC-30MB AND DISC-60MB AND DISC-90MB
 7   DISC-2-75MB (150MB)
10   DISC-21MB AND DISC-14MB

ENTER MSTYP: 2
REMOVABLE OR FIXED (R/F): F


 INITIALIZED FOR:

   DISC-10MB
   FIXED PACK



 WE REMIND YOU OF THE FOLLOWING MACM COMMANDS:

       )REDEF   => REDEFINE DISC TYPE
       )HENT    => GET SINTRAN FROM SAVE-AREA
       22!      => START SINTRAN
       10,0$    => LOAD SINTRAN FROM DISKETTE

 YOU MAY NOW TYPE ANY MACM COMMAND.
 ==================================


10,0$


%%  SINTRAN-III  VERSION H   LOAD:     SINTRAN-DISKETTE-I
%%  =======================================================

10,0$

%% DUMPING OF FILE-SYSTEM PART-1 DONE.
10,0$

10,0$

%% AND TYPE: 10,0$   TO CONTINUE LOADING
)9SBLO 4
